Create list of all possible pairs with distances\n    pairs = []\n    for i, centroid in enumerate(cluster_centroids):\n        for j, expert in enumerate(expert_points):\n            dist = euclidean(centroid, expert)\n            pairs.append((dist, i, j))\n\n    # pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN\nfrom scipy.spatial.distance import euclidean\nfrom itertools import product\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n\ndef custom_distance(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"Custom distance metric for DBSCAN.\"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx) ** 2 + ((2 - shape_weight) * dy) ** 2)\n\n\ndef custom_pairwise_distances(X, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"Compute pairwise distances using custom metric.\"\"\"\n    n = X.shape[0]\n    distances = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(i + 1, n):\n            dist = custom_distance(X[i], X[j], shape_weight)\n            distances[i, j] = dist\n            distances[j, i] = dist\n\n    return distances\n\n\nclass CustomDBSCAN:\n    \"\"\"DBSCAN with custom distance metric.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, eps, min_samples, shape_weight):\n        self.eps = eps\n        self.min_samples = min_samples\n        self.shape_weight = shape_weight\n\n    def fit_predict(self, X):\n        \"\"\"Fit DBSCAN and return cluster labels.\"\"\"\n        distances = custom_pairwise_distances(X, self.shape_weight)\n        dbscan = DBSCAN(eps=self.eps, min_samples=self.min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n        labels = dbscan.fit_predict(distances)\n        return labels\n\n\ndef greedy_match_clusters_to_experts(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_dist=100):\n    \"\"\"Match cluster centroids to expert points using greedy matching.\"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    used_experts = set()\n\n    pairs = []\n    for i, centroid in enumerate(cluster_centroids):\n        for j, expert in enumerate(expert_points):\n            dist = euclidean(centroid, expert)\n            pairs.append((dist, i, j))\n\n    pairs.sort()\n\n    for dist, centroid_idx, expert_idx in pairs:\n        if dist <= max_dist and expert_idx not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((centroid_idx, expert_idx, dist))\n            used_experts.add(expert_idx)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_and_delta(citsci_points, expert_points, labels, max_dist=100):\n    \"\"\"Compute F1 score and delta for a single image.\"\"\"\n    unique_labels = np.unique(labels)\n    cluster_labels = unique_labels[unique_labels != -1]\n\n    if len(cluster_labels) == 0 or len(expert_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    cluster_centroids = []\n    for cluster_id in cluster_labels:\n        cluster_points = citsci_points[labels == cluster_id]\n        centroid = np.mean(cluster_points, axis=0)\n        cluster_centroids.append(centroid)\n\n    cluster_centroids = np.array(cluster_centroids)\n    expert_points = np.array(expert_points)\n\n    matches = greedy_match_clusters_to_experts(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_dist)\n\n    n_matches = len(matches)\n    n_clusters = len(cluster_centroids)\n    n_experts = len(expert_points)\n\n    if n_matches == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    precision = n_matches / n_clusters if n_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = n_matches / n_experts if n_experts > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        f1 = 0.0\n    else:\n        f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)\n\n    delta = np.mean([match[2] for match in matches])\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_hyperparameters(params):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination.\"\"\"\n    min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, citsci_dict, expert_images = params\n\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    for file_rad in expert_images:\n        citsci_img = citsci_dict.get(file_rad, pd.DataFrame())\n\n        if len(citsci_img) == 0:\n            f1_scores.append(0.0)\n            deltas.append(np.nan)\n            continue\n\n        citsci_points = citsci_img[['x', 'y']].values\n        expert_points = np.array([[x, y] for x, y in citsci_img.iloc[0][['expert_x', 'expert_y']]])\n\n        if len(expert_points) == 0:\n            f1_scores.append(0.0)\n            deltas.append(np.nan)\n            continue\n\n        dbscan = CustomDBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, shape_weight=shape_weight)\n        labels = dbscan.fit_predict(citsci_points)\n\n        f1, delta = compute_f1_and_delta(citsci_points, expert_points, labels)\n\n        f1_scores.append(f1)\n        deltas.append(delta)\n\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n    avg_delta = np.nanmean(deltas)\n\n    return min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, avg_f1, avg_delta\n\n\ndef find_pareto_frontier(results):\n    \"\"\"Find Pareto-optimal points from results.\"\"\"\n    pareto_points = []\n\n    for i, point in enumerate(results):\n        is_pareto = True\n\n        for j, other_point in enumerate(results):\n            if i == j:\n                continue\n\n            if (other_point[3] >= point[3] and\n                other_point[4] <= point[4] and\n                (other_point[3] > point[3] or other_point[4] < point[4])):\n                is_pareto = False\n                break\n\n        if is_pareto:\n            pareto_points.append(point)\n\n    return pareto_points\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Citizen science annotations: {len(citsci_df)}\")\n    print(f\"Expert annotations: {len(expert_df)}\")\n    print(f\"Unique images in expert dataset: {expert_df['file_rad'].nunique()}\")\n\n    # Prepare data structure for faster lookup\n    citsci_dict = {}\n    expert_data = {}\n\n    for _, row in citsci_df.iterrows():\n        file_rad = row['file_rad']\n        if file_rad not in citsci_dict:\n            citsci_dict[file_rad] = []\n        citsci_dict[file_rad].append({'x': row['x'], 'y': row['y']})\n\n    for file_rad in citsci_dict:\n        citsci_dict[file_rad] = pd.DataFrame(citsci_dict[file_rad])\n\n    for _, row in expert_df.iterrows():\n        file_rad = row['file_rad']\n        if file_rad not in expert_data:\n            expert_data[file_rad] = []\n        expert_data[file_rad].append({'x': row['x'], 'y': row['y']})\n\n    # Get list of expert images\n    expert_images = list(expert_data.keys())\n\n    # Define hyperparameter grid\n    min_samples_range = range(3, 10)\n    epsilon_range = range(4, 25, 2)\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)\n\n    print(f\"\\nGrid search configuration:\")\n    print(f\"min_samples: {list(min_samples_range)}\")\n    print(f\"epsilon: {list(epsilon_range)}\")\n    print(f\"shape_weight: {list(np.round(shape_weight_range, 1))}\")\n    print(f\"Total combinations: {len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)}\")\n\n    # Prepare parameter combinations\n    param_combinations = []\n    for min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight in product(\n        min_samples_range, epsilon_range, shape_weight_range\n    ):\n        param_combinations.append((\n            min_samples,\n            round(epsilon, 1),\n            round(shape_weight, 1),\n            citsci_dict,\n            expert_images\n        ))\n\n    print(f\"\\nStarting grid search...\")\n    print(f\"This will evaluate {len(param_combinations)} combinations\")\n    print(f\"Estimated time: {len(param_combinations) * 12 / 3600:.1f} hours\")\n    print()\n\n    # Run grid search\n    all_results = []\n    total = len(param_combinations)\n\n    for i, params in enumerate(param_combinations):\n        if i % 10 == 0:\n            print(f\"Progress: {i}/{total} custom_distance(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"Custom distance metric for DBSCAN.\"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx) ** 2 + ((2 - shape_weight) * dy) ** 2)\n\n\ndef custom_pairwise_distances(X, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"Compute pairwise distances using custom metric.\"\"\"\n    n = X.shape[0]\n    distances = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(i + 1, n):\n            dist = custom_distance(X[i], X[j], shape_weight)\n            distances[i, j] = dist\n            distances[j, i] = dist\n\n    return self.shape_weight)\n        dbscan = DBSCAN(eps=self.eps, min_samples=self.min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n        labels = dbscan.fit_predict(distances)\n        return labels\n\n\ndef custom_distance(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"Custom distance metric for DBSCAN.\"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx) ** 2 + ((2 - shape_weight) pairs.append((dist, i, j))\n\n    pairs.sort()\n\n    for dist, centroid_idx, expert_idx in pairs:\n        if dist <= max_dist and expert_idx not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((centroid_idx, expert_idx, dist))\n            used_experts.add(expert_idx)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_and_delta(citsci_points, expert_points, labels, max_dist=100):\n    \"\"\"Compute F1 score and delta for a single image.\"\"\"\n    unique_labels = np.unique(labels)\n    cluster_labels = unique_labels[unique_labels != -1]\n\n    if len(cluster_labels) == 0 or len(expert_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    cluster_centroids = []\n    for cluster_id in cluster_labels:\n        cluster_points = citsci_points[labels == cluster_id]\n        centroid = np.mean(cluster_points, axis=0)\n        cluster_centroids.append(centroid)\n\n    cluster_centroids = np.array(cluster_centroids)\n    expert_points = np.array(expert_points)\n\n    matches = greedy_match_clusters_to_experts(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_dist)\n\n    n_matches = len(matches)\n    n_clusters = len(cluster_centroids)\n    n_experts = len(expert_points)\n\n    if n_matches == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    precision = n_matches / n_clusters if n_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = n_matches / n_experts if n_experts > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        f1 = 0.0\n    else:\n        f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)\n\n    delta = np.mean([match[2] for match in matches])\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_single_combination(min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, citsci_df, expert_df):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination.\"\"\"\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n custom_distance(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"Custom distance metric for DBSCAN.\"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx) ** 2 + ((2 - shape_weight)
